---
answers:
- accepted: true
  body_markdown: "With C/C++ errors, it typically suffices to check the first error.\
    \ In this case that would be:\n\n>     In file included from /usr/include/c++/5/random:35:0,\n\
    \                     from /usr/include/ignition/math2/ignition/math/Rand.hh:20,\n\
    \                     from /usr/include/ignition/math2/ignition/math.hh:18,\n\
    \                     from /usr/include/sdformat-4.0/sdf/Param.hh:34,\n      \
    \               from /usr/include/sdformat-4.0/sdf/Element.hh:24,\n          \
    \           from /usr/include/sdformat-4.0/sdf/sdf.hh:5,\n                   \
    \  from /usr/include/gazebo-7/gazebo/common/Plugin.hh:42,\n                  \
    \   from /home/metobom/m3_ws/src/evarobot_simulator/evarobot_gazebo/include/plugins/im_gazebo_ros_sonar.h:32,\n\
    \                     from /home/metobom/m3_ws/src/evarobot_simulator/evarobot_gazebo/src/im_gazebo_ros_sonar.cpp:29:\n\
    \    /usr/include/c++/5/bits/c++0x_warning.h:32:2: error: #error This file requires\
    \ compiler and library support for the ISO C++ 2011 standard. This support must\
    \ be enabled with the -std=c++11 or -std=gnu++11 compiler options.\n     #error\
    \ This file requires compiler and library support \\\n\nwhich tells us that `evarobot_simulator/evarobot_gazebo`\
    \ (or one of its direct dependencies) appears to require a C++11 enabled compiler.\n\
    \nAs you tagged this `kinetic`, I assume you are running this on Ubuntu Xenial,\
    \ which comes with GCC 5.4 by default, which afaik doesn't enable C++11 automatically.\n\
    \nPerhaps `evarobot_simulator/evarobot_gazebo` should enable C++11 in its build\
    \ scripts (ie: `CMakeLists.txt`), but that would be something to ask the developers\
    \ / maintainers.\n\n---\n\nEdit: according to [this tutorial](http://wiki.ros.org/Robots/evarobot/Tutorials/indigo/PC%20Installation),\
    \ the instructions are for Ubuntu Indigo:\n\n> These instructions will guide you\
    \ through installing software and configuring your PC to communicate with the\
    \ evarobot. You should install Ubuntu 14.04 operating system and ROS Indigo to\
    \ your PC. \n\nC++11 would not be enabled there by default either, but it could\
    \ be that the requirement for C++11 comes from one of the *dependencies of the\
    \ evarobot packages*, in which case changing to a newer platform could have introduced\
    \ that without the evarobot packages themselves having changed.\n\n---\n\nEdit\
    \ 2: and according to [inomuh/evarobot_simulator#1](https://github.com/inomuh/evarobot_simulator/issues/1),\
    \ changes are needed to the various packages before they will build on Kinetic.\n\
    \nI'd ask the developers whether they support Ubuntu Xenial + ROS Kinetic."
  comments:
  - body_markdown: |-
      How can I compile with C++11? Is there any way to do it? And I use Ubuntu 16.04 LTS.
    creation_date: '2019-01-23T08:06:39Z'
    original_post_id: 313525
    owner_display_name: metobom
    owner_id: 39588
    owner_karma: 27
    owner_profile_url: https://answers.ros.org/users/39588/metobom/
  - body_markdown: |-
      I would expect the developers of the `evarobot` pkgs to have configured things correctly for you, so I would make sure you are using a supported platform first, before trying to change things.

      re: Ubuntu 16.04: that is Ubuntu Xenial.
    creation_date: '2019-01-23T08:08:46Z'
    original_post_id: 313526
    owner_display_name: gvdhoorn
    owner_id: 5184
    owner_karma: 86574
    owner_profile_url: https://answers.ros.org/users/5184/gvdhoorn/
  - body_markdown: |-
      Thanks a lot I managed. I followed https://github.com/inomuh/evarobot_simulator/issues/1.
    creation_date: '2019-01-23T09:17:24Z'
